BOOTSTRAP READ ORDER — MINIMUM
0. Read this small static FASTPATH if needed. On normal restart using the literal stage37mainbatch token, it suffices to use this fixed procedure without searching for fresh startup docs.
1. Open ONLY MAIN's FIRST CURRENT AUTHORITY OVERRIDE block, starting at document beginning and ending BEFORE the next CURRENT AUTHORITY OVERRIDE heading. Extract CANONICAL_CURRENT_POINTER, CANONICAL_CURRENT_DOC_ID, STARTUP/active-route, CURRENT_STAGE37_GOAL, NEXT_RESEARCH_INPUT_CONTRACT, PRIMARY_GATE, credit/closure boundaries. Treat all subsequent older current blocks as HISTORY. If connector returns whole MAIN, extract top current block inside the tooling layer and emit ONLY this bounded block to chat; never dump its 180k+ characters into model context. If no valid top block or ambiguous ID, STOP and identify the mismatch.
2. CARD-INDEX: retrieve only the row matching exact CANONICAL_CURRENT_POINTER or exact current DOC ID. Use targeted bounded native Sheet range/search, not the entire workbook or hundreds of rows. Do not hardcode 'row586': row numbers advance. Confirm matched row's card ID and status.
3. RESULT-LEDGER: read only the FIRST CURRENT Stage37 CHANGE RECORD block (or current header variant) and verify CANONICAL_CURRENT_POINTER/DOC_ID match MAIN. If connector returns whole cumulative ledger, extract its bounded first block before emitting any text to chat. Do not scan older entries absent an explicit named dependency.
4. ACTIVE ROUTE: read only its FIRST CURRENT AUTHORITY block up to the second CURRENT AUTHORITY heading. Verify latest card name/doc ID and same NEXT. The ROUTE is a cumulative append-only document, potentially over one million characters: NEVER inject whole content into model context, even to find current.
5. Read the single latest research-card via the MAIN canonical current DOC ID. Open only the exact 1–3 older source cards named as load-bearing dependencies WHEN the current proof/research leaf requires them. Fetch full evidence/verifiers/code only for a named claim/source replay; otherwise don't read.
6. Perform substantive research on the real NEXT and preserve the original full physical masks, selectors, positivity, primitivity, R-height, and all gates. New substantive result: create standalone readable research-card; update CARD-INDEX, RESULT-LEDGER, MAIN first current override, active ROUTE first current block; read back each edited authority + card. Do not append extra redundant startup history or full historical recaps for a merely operational change.
CURRENT SYNC RULE
All four live heads and current card must resolve to ONE same current pointer. If concurrency produces a newer head while working, re-read the heads and reconcile; do not overwrite a newer mainbatch result, fabricate missing index rows or revert a new proof. MAIN top is current mathematical-operational authority; index/ledger/route are projections that must be reconciled at each completed substantive batch.
If headings change, find only the current-top delimiters in tooling output, and fail closed for a genuinely ambiguous head. Never infer authority from global string search, old card title, prior chat memory, an archival copy, or largest document content.
AVOID UNNECESSARY READS
- Entire historical MAIN, RESULT-LEDGER, active ROUTE (aggregate text >1.7 million characters).
- Whole CARD-INDEX sheet instead of one current row.
- Older research cards, prior sections, legacy full archive/index, prior audits, large proof bundles, GitHub.
- Re-reading previously PASSed algebra or old verifiers on every ordinary restart.
- Duplicate source descriptions already present in the current latest research-card.
Full audit or rollback may request exact historical evidence independently; this fastpath does not weaken mathematical verification when it is actually needed.
LIMITATION
The available Google Docs native connector may transfer the full document response to the tool runtime when reading these append-only docs. Filtering FIRST inside tool code reduces model-context text and unnecessary reasoning cost, but does not by itself guarantee reduced upstream Google Docs API transfer. A future physically compact live-head document could reduce API transfer only if it is kept transactionally synchronized with MAIN. Do not introduce a second, potentially stale mutable mathematical authority without an explicit synchronization/readback protocol.
